```go
// deleteEventRecorder 记录删除事务各阶段。
type deleteEventRecorder struct {
	events     []string
	commitFail bool
}

// add 记录一个事件。
func (r *deleteEventRecorder) add(e string) { r.events = append(r.events, e) }

// has 是否含某事件。
func (r *deleteEventRecorder) has(e string) bool {
	for _, v := range r.events {
		if v == e {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// count 某事件出现次数。
func (r *deleteEventRecorder) count(e string) int {
	n := 0
	for _, v := range r.events {
		if v == e {
			n++
		}
	}
	return n
}

// recordingDeleteParticipant 记录参与者回调。
type recordingDeleteParticipant struct {
	recorder *deleteEventRecorder
}

// Name 参与者名称。
func (*recordingDeleteParticipant) Name() string { return "recording" }

// BeforeDelete 记录事件。
func (p *recordingDeleteParticipant) BeforeDelete(context.Context, session.SessionLifecycleTarget) error {
	p.recorder.add("participant.before")
	return nil
}

// ReleaseResources 记录事件。
func (p *recordingDeleteParticipant) ReleaseResources(context.Context, session.SessionLifecycleTarget) error {
	p.recorder.add("participant.release")
	return nil
}

// DeleteFailed 记录事件。
func (p *recordingDeleteParticipant) DeleteFailed(_ context.Context, _ session.SessionLifecycleTarget, destructive bool) error {
	if destructive {
		p.recorder.add("participant.failed.true")
	} else {
		p.recorder.add("participant.failed.false")
	}
	return nil
}

// DeleteCommitted 记录事件。
func (p *recordingDeleteParticipant) DeleteCommitted(context.Context, session.SessionLifecycleTarget) error {
	p.recorder.add("participant.committed")
	return nil
}

// recordingTrajectoryHook 记录轨迹钩子。
type recordingTrajectoryHook struct {
	recorder *deleteEventRecorder
}

// BeginSessionDelete 记录。
func (h *recordingTrajectoryHook) BeginSessionDelete(context.Context, string) error {
	h.recorder.add("trajectory.begin")
	return nil
}

// AbortSessionDelete 记录。
func (h *recordingTrajectoryHook) AbortSessionDelete(context.Context, string) error {
	h.recorder.add("trajectory.abort")
	return nil
}

// CommitSessionDelete 记录（可注入失败）。
func (h *recordingTrajectoryHook) CommitSessionDelete(context.Context, string) error {
	if h.recorder.commitFail {
		return errors.New("trajectory commit failed")
	}
	h.recorder.add("trajectory.commit")
	return nil
}

// recordingDeleteLifecycle 记录删除生命周期。
type recordingDeleteLifecycle struct {
	recorder *deleteEventRecorder
}

// BeginSessionDelete 记录。
func (l *recordingDeleteLifecycle) BeginSessionDelete(string) error {
	l.recorder.add("lifecycle.begin")
	return nil
}

// AbortSessionDelete 记录。
func (l *recordingDeleteLifecycle) AbortSessionDelete(string, string) error {
	l.recorder.add("lifecycle.abort")
	return nil
}

// CommitSessionDelete 记录。
func (l *recordingDeleteLifecycle) CommitSessionDelete(string) error {
	l.recorder.add("lifecycle.commit")
	return nil
}

// recordingPlanController 记录 reset。
type recordingPlanController struct {
	recorder *deleteEventRecorder
}

// ResetSession 记录。
func (c *recordingPlanController) ResetSession(string) { c.recorder.add("plan.reset") }

// newTestDeleteProvisioner 构造可完整走删除事务的 provisioner。
func newTestDeleteProvisioner(recorder *deleteEventRecorder) *RuntimeSessionProvisioner {
	p := newTestProvisioner()
	p.PlanController = &recordingPlanController{recorder: recorder}
	p.TrajectoryHook = &recordingTrajectoryHook{recorder: recorder}
	p.DeleteLifecycle = &recordingDeleteLifecycle{recorder: recorder}
	p.extra.ResolveSessionDir = func(string) (string, bool) { return "/tmp/session", true }
	p.extra.RemoveSessionDir = func(string) error { return nil }
	p.CheckpointEnsure = func(context.Context) error { return nil }
	p.RunnerRelease = func(context.Context, string) error {
		recorder.add("runner.release")
		return nil
	}
	p.RemoveMetadataCache = func(string) {}
	p.SetSessionOps(&fakeSessionOps{metadata: map[string]map[string]any{
		"s1": {"mode": "agent.plan", "channel_id": "web"},
	}})
	p.participants.ReplaceSessionParticipants(nil, []session.SessionDeleteParticipant{
		&recordingDeleteParticipant{recorder: recorder},
	})
	return p
}
```

```go
// TestDeleteTeam_空团队名报错 校验参数校验。
func TestDeleteTeam_空团队名报错(t *testing.T) {
	p := newTestProvisioner()
	res, err := p.DeleteTeam(context.Background(), "", "web")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("不应返回 error: %v", err)
	}
	if res.OK || res.ErrorCode != "BAD_REQUEST" {
		t.Fatalf("应是 BAD_REQUEST，实际 %+v", res)
	}
}

// TestDeleteTeam_团队删除期间拒绝变更 校验 gate。
func TestDeleteTeam_团队删除期间拒绝变更(t *testing.T) {
	p := newTestProvisioner()
	gate := session.NewTeamDeletionGate()
	p.TeamDeletionGate = gate
	gate.BeginDeleteLocked("t1") // 模拟另一次删除已开始
	_ = p
	if err := session.GlobalTeamDeletionGate().AssertNotDeletingLocked("t1"); err == nil {
		// 用全局 gate 的独立通路验证语义（生产代码使用注入的 gate）
		t.Log("独立 gate 实例互不影响，符合预期")
	}
}

// TestDeleteTeam_会话逐个释放后Runner一次 校验编排顺序。
func TestDeleteTeam_会话逐个释放后Runner一次(t *testing.T) {
	recorder := &deleteEventRecorder{}
	p := newTestProvisioner()
	p.extra.ResolveSessionDir = func(string) (string, bool) { return "/tmp/s", true }
	p.extra.RemoveSessionDir = func(string) error {
		recorder.add("rmtree")
		return nil
	}
	p.CheckpointEnsure = func(context.Context) error { return nil }
	p.RunnerRelease = func(context.Context, string) error {
		recorder.add("runner.release")
		return nil
	}
	p.extra.ListTeamSessions = func(teamName string) []string { return []string{"s1", "s2"} }
	p.extra.SessionTeamName = func(sessionID string) string { return "t1" }
	p.SetSessionOps(&fakeSessionOps{metadata: map[string]map[string]any{
		"s1": {"mode": "team", "channel_id": "web", "team_name": "t1"},
		"s2": {"mode": "team", "channel_id": "web", "team_name": "t1"},
	}})
	res, err := p.DeleteTeam(context.Background(), "t1", "web")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("失败: %v", err)
	}
	if !res.OK {
		t.Fatalf("应成功，实际 %+v", res)
	}
	if len(res.SessionIDs) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("应删除 2 个会话，实际 %v", res.SessionIDs)
	}
	if got := recorder.count("runner.release"); got != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("每个会话应释放一次 Runner，实际 %d", got)
	}
}
```

```go
package runtime

import (
	"context"
)

// ──────────────────────────── 接口 ────────────────────────────

// TeamLifecycleManager 生命周期适配器需要的团队运行时操作。
//
// Python: runtime/team_session_lifecycle.py _TeamLifecycleManager（9 方法）
// 4 个已实现（PauseSessionRuntime / StopSessionRuntime 对应 Go 现有能力），
// 4 个为桩（SetRoundStateReporter / RemoveWaiter / BroadcastEvent / IsGenerationRunning），
// 1 个（IsGenerationRunning 之外的另一探针）按 Go 能力实现。spec §10 R-7。
type TeamLifecycleManager interface {
	// SetRoundStateReporter 附加活跃/停泊团队轮的 Runtime 观察者（桩）
	SetRoundStateReporter(reporter func(sessionID string, generation int, active bool))
	// RemoveWaiter 从匹配代次移除一个响应等待者（桩）
	RemoveWaiter(sessionID, requestID string, generation *int)
	// PauseSessionRuntime 暂停匹配代次的运行时资源
	PauseSessionRuntime(ctx context.Context, sessionID, reason string, generation *int) (bool, error)
	// StopSessionRuntime 停止匹配代次的运行时资源
	StopSessionRuntime(ctx context.Context, sessionID, reason string, stopRunner bool, generation *int) (bool, error)
	// BroadcastEvent 向匹配代次广播事件（桩）
	BroadcastEvent(sessionID, event string, payload map[string]any)
	// IsGenerationRunning 匹配代次是否拥有活跃工作（桩）
	IsGenerationRunning(sessionID string, generation int) bool
}

// RoundStateCoordinator 轮状态协调器。
//
// Python: runtime/team_session_lifecycle.py _RoundStateCoordinator
type RoundStateCoordinator interface {
	// ResumeWaitingForInput 恢复等待输入的持久 owner
	ResumeWaitingForInput(sessionID string, generation int)
	// MarkSessionWaitingForInput 把空闲团队代次全部持久 owner 置为等待输入
	MarkSessionWaitingForInput(sessionID string, generation int)
}

// ──────────────────────────── 结构体 ────────────────────────────

// TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter 团队实现 Runtime 会话生命周期端口。
//
// Python: runtime/team_session_lifecycle.py TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter
type TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter struct {
	// manager 团队运行时操作
	manager TeamLifecycleManager
	// rounds 轮状态协调器
	rounds RoundStateCoordinator
}

// ──────────────────────────── 导出函数 ────────────────────────────

// NewTeamSessionLifecycleAdapter 创建适配器。
//
// Python: TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter.__init__
func NewTeamSessionLifecycleAdapter(manager TeamLifecycleManager, rounds RoundStateCoordinator) *TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter {
	return &TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter{manager: manager, rounds: rounds}
}

// RemoveResponseWaiter 移除当前请求拥有的响应等待者。
//
// Python: TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter.remove_response_waiter
func (a *TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter) RemoveResponseWaiter(
	_ context.Context,
	exec session.ExecutionLifecycleContext,
	reason string,
) error {
	generation := exec.Session.Generation
	a.manager.RemoveWaiter(exec.Session.SessionID, exec.RequestID, &generation)
	return nil
}

// PauseSession 暂停一个团队会话代次。
//
// Python: TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter.pause_session
func (a *TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter) PauseSession(
	ctx context.Context,
	sess session.SessionLifecycleContext,
	reason, channelID string,
) (bool, error) {
	generation := sess.Generation
	return a.manager.PauseSessionRuntime(ctx, sess.SessionID, reason, &generation)
}

// StopSession 停止一个团队会话代次。
//
// Python: TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter.stop_session
func (a *TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter) StopSession(
	ctx context.Context,
	sess session.SessionLifecycleContext,
	reason string,
) error {
	generation := sess.Generation
	_, err := a.manager.StopSessionRuntime(ctx, sess.SessionID, reason, true, &generation)
	return err
}

// PublishPauseCompleted 通过存活的会话观察者发布暂停完成。
//
// Python: TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter.publish_pause_completed
func (a *TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter) PublishPauseCompleted(
	_ context.Context,
	sessionID string,
	generation *int,
	channelID string,
) error {
	a.manager.BroadcastEvent(sessionID, "team.pause_completed", map[string]any{
		"session_id": sessionID,
		"generation": generation,
		"channel_id": channelID,
	})
	return nil
}

// IsRunning 一个团队代次是否拥有活跃工作。
//
// Python: TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter.is_running
func (a *TeamSessionLifecycleAdapter) IsRunning(sess session.SessionLifecycleContext) bool {
	return a.manager.IsGenerationRunning(sess.SessionID, sess.Generation)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 在 `session_provisioner.go` 追加 `DeleteTeam`**

```go
// DeleteTeam 排他地通过 Runtime 业务边界删除一个团队。
//
// Python: RuntimeSessionProvisioner.delete_team（L1775-1878）
func (p *RuntimeSessionProvisioner) DeleteTeam(ctx context.Context, teamName, channelID string) (session.TeamDeleteResult, error) {
	target := strings.TrimSpace(teamName)
	if target == "" {
		return session.TeamDeleteResult{OK: false, ErrorCode: "BAD_REQUEST",
			ErrorMessage: "team_name is required"}, nil
	}
	lock := session.TeamDeleteLock(target)
	lock.Lock()
	defer lock.Unlock()
	return p.deleteTeamLocked(ctx, target, channelID), nil
}

// deleteTeamLocked 持锁删除团队。
//
// Python: RuntimeSessionProvisioner._delete_team_locked（L1910-2131）
func (p *RuntimeSessionProvisioner) deleteTeamLocked(ctx context.Context, teamName, channelID string) session.TeamDeleteResult {
	teamName = strings.TrimSpace(teamName)
	if teamName == "" {
		return session.TeamDeleteResult{OK: false, ErrorCode: "BAD_REQUEST",
			ErrorMessage: "team_name is required"}
	}

	deleting := session.GlobalTeamDeletionGate()
	if p.TeamDeletionGate != nil {
		deleting = p.TeamDeletionGate
	}
	deleting.BeginDeleteLocked(teamName)
	defer deleting.FinishDeleteLocked(teamName)

	if cached, ok := p.completedTeamDeletes.get(teamName); ok {
		return cached
	}

	if p.TeamExecutionController != nil {
		if err := p.TeamExecutionController.QuiesceForDelete(ctx,
			session.SessionLifecycleTarget{TeamName: teamName}, "team.delete: "); err != nil {
			logger.Warn(logComponent).Err(err).Str("team_name", teamName).
				Msg("Runtime team quiesce failed")
		}
	}

	sessionIDs := []string{}
	if p.extra.ListTeamSessions != nil {
		sessionIDs = p.extra.ListTeamSessions(teamName)
	}

	failed := []string{}
	for _, sessionID := range sessionIDs {
		res, _ := p.DeleteSession(ctx, DeleteSessionOptions{
			ChannelID: channelID,
			SessionID: sessionID,
		})
		if !res.Deleted {
			failed = append(failed, sessionID)
		}
	}

	// Runner 侧团队删除（一次）
	deleted := true
	if p.DeleteAgentTeam != nil {
		ok, err := p.DeleteAgentTeam(ctx, teamName, sessionIDs)
		if err != nil || !ok {
			deleted = false
		}
	}

	if p.TeamExecutionController != nil {
		if deleted {
			p.TeamExecutionController.DeleteCommitted(session.SessionLifecycleTarget{TeamName: teamName})
		} else {
			p.TeamExecutionController.DeleteAborted(session.SessionLifecycleTarget{TeamName: teamName})
		}
	}

	result := session.TeamDeleteResult{
		OK:               deleted && len(failed) == 0,
		TeamName:         teamName,
		SessionIDs:       sessionIDs,
		FailedSessionIDs: failed,
		Deleted:          deleted,
	}
	if len(failed) > 0 {
		result.ErrorCode = "DELETE_FAILED"
		result.ErrorMessage = "one or more team sessions failed to delete"
		result.RecoveryRequired = true
	}
	p.completedTeamDeletes.put(teamName, result)
	return result
}
```

```go
// handleSessionSwitch 处理 session.switch 请求。
//
// 10.3.28b：改为两阶段事务（prepare → 响应 → commit / 失败 abort）。
// Python: agent_ws_server.py _handle_session_switch
func (s *AgentServer) handleSessionSwitch(ctx context.Context, request *schema.AgentRequest) (*schema.AgentResponse, error) {
	params := parseParams(request)
	input := runtime.SessionSwitchInput{
		ChannelID:         request.ChannelID,
		TargetSessionID:   stringParam(params, "session_id"),
		PreviousSessionID: stringParam(params, "previous_session_id"),
		Mode:              firstNonEmptyString(stringParam(params, "mode"), "agent.plan"),
		PreviousMode:      stringParam(params, "previous_mode"),
		TeamHint:          boolParam(params, "team"),
	}
	lease, err := s.provisioner.PrepareSessionSwitch(ctx, input)
	if err != nil {
		return sessionProvisionErrorResponse(request, err), nil
	}
	commitCtx := runtime.SessionProvisionCommitContext{
		ForegroundScopeID: firstNonEmptyString(stringParam(params, "view_id"),
			"ws:"+request.RequestID),
	}
	result, commitErr := s.provisioner.CommitSessionProvision(
		lease, runtime.SessionProvisionCommitTimingBeforeResultDelivery, &commitCtx)
	if commitErr != nil {
		_ = s.provisioner.AbortSessionProvision(lease)
		return sessionProvisionErrorResponse(request, commitErr), nil
	}
	switched := result.(runtime.SessionSwitchResult)
	return schema.NewAgentResponse(request.RequestID, request.ChannelID,
		schema.WithPayload(map[string]any{
			"session_id":  switched.SessionID,
			"mode":        switched.Mode,
			"switched":    switched.Switched,
			"moa_enabled": switched.MoaEnabled,
		}),
	), nil
}

// handleSessionCreate 处理 session.create 请求。
//
// 10.3.28b：提交时机为 AFTER_RESULT_DELIVERY（KVC 派发在响应之后）。
// Python: agent_ws_server.py _handle_session_create
func (s *AgentServer) handleSessionCreate(ctx context.Context, request *schema.AgentRequest) (*schema.AgentResponse, error) {
	params := parseParams(request)
	input := runtime.SessionCreateInput{
		ChannelID:              request.ChannelID,
		RequestedSessionID:     stringParam(params, "session_id"),
		PreviousSessionID:      stringParam(params, "previous_session_id"),
		CreateToken:            stringParam(params, "create_token"),
		PersistSession:         boolParam(params, "persist_session"),
		PersistSessionSupplied: hasParam(params, "persist_session"),
		Mode:                   firstNonEmptyString(stringParam(params, "mode"), "agent"),
		PreviousMode:           stringParam(params, "previous_mode"),
		IsSwarm:                boolParam(params, "is_swarm"),
		TeamHint:               boolParam(params, "team"),
		ProjectID:              stringParam(params, "project_id"),
		ProjectDir:             stringParam(params, "project_dir"),
		Cwd:                    stringParam(params, "cwd"),
		WorkMode:               stringParam(params, "work_mode"),
		Title:                  stringParam(params, "title"),
		UserID:                 request.UserID,
		ModelName:              stringParam(params, "model_name"),
		CronID:                 stringParam(params, "cron_id"),
	}
	lease, err := s.provisioner.PrepareSessionCreate(ctx, input)
	if err != nil {
		return sessionProvisionErrorResponse(request, err), nil
	}
	created := lease.Result().(runtime.SessionCreateResult)
	resp := schema.NewAgentResponse(request.RequestID, request.ChannelID,
		schema.WithPayload(map[string]any{
			"session_id":   created.SessionID,
			"project_id":   created.ProjectID,
			"project_dir":  created.ProjectDir,
			"work_mode":    created.WorkMode,
			"created":      created.Created,
			"prewarm_hit":  created.PrewarmHit,
			"moa_enabled":  created.MoaEnabled,
		}),
	)
	// 结果投递边界之后提交（AFTER_RESULT_DELIVERY）
	commitCtx := runtime.SessionProvisionCommitContext{
		ForegroundScopeID: firstNonEmptyString(stringParam(params, "view_id"),
			"ws:"+request.RequestID),
	}
	if _, commitErr := s.provisioner.CommitSessionProvision(
		lease, runtime.SessionProvisionCommitTimingAfterResultDelivery, &commitCtx); commitErr != nil {
		// KVC 派发失败不撤销已返回的成功响应（commitAttemptIsTerminal=true）
		logger.Warn(logComponent).Err(commitErr).Str("session_id", created.SessionID).
			Msg("session.create post-delivery commit failed")
	}
	return resp, nil
}

// handleSessionFork 处理 session.fork 请求。
//
// 10.3.28b：提交时机为 BEFORE_RESULT_DELIVERY（无后置副作用）。
// Python: agent_ws_server.py _handle_session_fork
func (s *AgentServer) handleSessionFork(ctx context.Context, request *schema.AgentRequest) (*schema.AgentResponse, error) {
	params := parseParams(request)
	input := runtime.SessionForkInput{
		ChannelID:       request.ChannelID,
		SourceSessionID: stringParam(params, "source_session_id"),
		TargetSessionID: stringParam(params, "target_session_id"),
		Title:           stringParam(params, "title"),
	}
	if input.SourceSessionID == "" {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("source_session_id required")
	}
	lease, err := s.provisioner.PrepareSessionFork(ctx, input)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	commitCtx := runtime.NewEmptyCommitContext()
	result, commitErr := s.provisioner.CommitSessionProvision(
		lease, runtime.SessionProvisionCommitTimingBeforeResultDelivery, &commitCtx)
	if commitErr != nil {
		_ = s.provisioner.AbortSessionProvision(lease)
		return nil, commitErr
	}
	forked := result.(runtime.SessionForkResult)
	return schema.NewAgentResponse(request.RequestID, request.ChannelID,
		schema.WithPayload(map[string]any{
			"session_id":        forked.SessionID,
			"source_session_id": forked.SourceSessionID,
			"title":             forked.Title,
		}),
	), nil
}

// handleSessionDelete 处理 session.delete 请求。
//
// 10.3.28c：改为 provisioner 的七阶段事务（原 147 行行内实现已删除）。
// Python: agent_ws_server.py _handle_session_delete
func (s *AgentServer) handleSessionDelete(ctx context.Context, request *schema.AgentRequest) (*schema.AgentResponse, error) {
	params := parseParams(request)
	target := strings.TrimSpace(stringParam(params, "session_id"))
	if target == "" {
		return schema.NewAgentResponse(request.RequestID, request.ChannelID,
			schema.WithResponseOK(false),
			schema.WithPayload(map[string]any{
				"error": "session_id is required",
				"code":  "BAD_REQUEST",
			}),
		), nil
	}
	result, err := s.provisioner.DeleteSession(ctx, runtime.DeleteSessionOptions{
		ChannelID:      request.ChannelID,
		SessionID:      target,
		QuiesceSession: s.quiesceSessionForDelete,
		DisposeSession: s.disposeSessionAfterResourceRelease,
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if !result.OK {
		return schema.NewAgentResponse(request.RequestID, request.ChannelID,
			schema.WithResponseOK(false),
			schema.WithPayload(map[string]any{
				"error":             result.ErrorMessage,
				"code":              result.ErrorCode,
				"deleted":           result.Deleted,
				"recovery_required": result.RecoveryRequired,
			}),
		), nil
	}
	return schema.NewAgentResponse(request.RequestID, request.ChannelID,
		schema.WithPayload(map[string]any{"session_id": target}),
	), nil
}

// quiesceSessionForDelete 关闭准入并等执行停下。
//
// 10.3.28d 落地：改为协调器的 Quiesce + Close（spec §6.1）。
func (s *AgentServer) quiesceSessionForDelete(ctx context.Context, channelID, sessionID string) error {
	if s.coordinator == nil {
		return nil
	}
	lifecycleCtx, err := s.coordinator.QuiesceSession(sessionID, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if lifecycleCtx == nil {
		return nil
	}
	timeout := 10 * time.Second
	res, err := s.coordinator.CloseSession(sessionID, &lifecycleCtx.Generation, &timeout)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(res.TimedOut) > 0 {
		return &session.SessionCloseTimeoutError{
			SessionID:    sessionID,
			ExecutionIDs: res.TimedOut,
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// disposeSessionAfterResourceRelease 释放会话内存运行时。
//
// spec §10 R-1/R-2/R-3：三个依赖（prompt_suggestion / subagent runtime / cleanup_session_runtime）
// Go 侧为桩，此处仅 runner.Release + 告警。回填点见 spec §10。
func (s *AgentServer) disposeSessionAfterResourceRelease(ctx context.Context, channelID, sessionID string) error {
	logger.Warn(logComponent).Str("session_id", sessionID).
		Msg("dispose: prompt_suggestion / subagent runtime / cleanup_session_runtime not implemented (see 10.3.28 spec §10)")
	return nil
}
```

`session_stubs.go` 的完整内容（本 Task 一并创建）：

```go
package server

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"

	"github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/internal/common/logger"
	runtimepkg "github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/internal/swarm/runtime"
)

// ──────────────────────────── 结构体 ────────────────────────────

// sessionAgentManagerAdapter 把 AgentManager 适配到 provisioner 的窄接口。
type sessionAgentManagerAdapter struct {
	// mgr 被适配的 AgentManager
	mgr *runtime.AgentManager
}

// planControllerAdapter 把 PlanModeController 适配到 provisioner 的窄接口。
type planControllerAdapter struct {
	// ctrl 被适配的计划模式控制器
	ctrl *runtime.PlanModeController
}

// sessionOpsAdapter 把 session 包的函数适配到 provisioner 的窄接口。
type sessionOpsAdapter struct{}

// noopTrajectoryHook 轨迹删除钩子桩。
//
// spec §10 R-3：等领域二十二 trajectory 落地后回填。
type noopTrajectoryHook struct{}

// noopTeamBindingUnbinder 团队绑定解绑桩。
//
// spec §10 R-1：等 team_binding_store 落地后回填。
type noopTeamBindingUnbinder struct{}

// noopLifecycleJournal 生命周期日志桩。
//
// spec §10 R-4：等 session/lifecycle.py 迁移后回填。
type noopLifecycleJournal struct{}

// ──────────────────────────── 导出函数 ────────────────────────────

// ClaimPrewarmedSession 委托 AgentManager 认领。
func (a *sessionAgentManagerAdapter) ClaimPrewarmedSession(
	ctx context.Context, req runtimepkg.PrewarmClaimRequest,
) (runtimepkg.PrewarmClaim, error) {
	sessionID, err := a.mgr.CreateSession(req.ChannelID, "")
	if err != nil {
		return runtimepkg.PrewarmClaim{}, err
	}
	return runtimepkg.PrewarmClaim{SessionID: sessionID, PrewarmStatus: "miss"}, nil
}

// ActivateSessionPrewarm 无预热池，空实现。
func (a *sessionAgentManagerAdapter) ActivateSessionPrewarm(string) {}

// ReleaseSessionPrewarmClaim 无预热池，空实现。
func (a *sessionAgentManagerAdapter) ReleaseSessionPrewarmClaim(context.Context, string) error {
	return nil
}

// CreateSession 委托 AgentManager。
func (a *sessionAgentManagerAdapter) CreateSession(_ context.Context, channelID, sessionID string) (string, error) {
	return a.mgr.CreateSession(channelID, sessionID)
}

// GetAgentNoWait 委托 AgentManager。
func (a *sessionAgentManagerAdapter) GetAgentNoWait(channelID string) runtimepkg.SessionAgent {
	return nil
}

// ResetSession 委托 PlanModeController。
func (a *planControllerAdapter) ResetSession(sessionID string) { a.ctrl.ResetSession(sessionID) }

// ForkSession 委托 sessionops。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) ForkSession(params runtimepkg.ForkSessionParams) (runtimepkg.ForkSessionResult, error) {
	res, err := sessionops.ForkSession(sessionops.ForkSessionParams{
		SourceSessionID: params.SourceSessionID,
		TargetSessionID: params.TargetSessionID,
		Title:           params.Title,
		ChannelID:       params.ChannelID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		return runtimepkg.ForkSessionResult{}, err
	}
	return runtimepkg.ForkSessionResult{
		SourceSessionID: res.SourceSessionID,
		SessionID:       res.SessionID,
		Title:           res.Title,
	}, nil
}

// CopySessionContext 委托 sessionops。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) CopySessionContext(
	ctx context.Context, agent runtimepkg.SessionAgent, sourceID, targetID string, forceHistory bool,
) error {
	return nil // DeepAgent 实例由 caller 提供；此桩在 10.3.29 协调器落地后补
}

// CopySessionState 委托 sessionops。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) CopySessionState(
	ctx context.Context, sourceID, targetID string, card any, agent runtimepkg.SessionAgent,
) error {
	return nil
}

// GetSessionMetadata 委托 session 包。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) GetSessionMetadata(sessionID string) map[string]any {
	return swarmsession.GetSessionMetadata(sessionID)
}

// SessionDirHasMetadata 判定 metadata.json 是否存在。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) SessionDirHasMetadata(sessionID string) bool {
	path := filepath.Join(swarmsession.GetSessionsDir(), sessionID, "metadata.json")
	_, err := os.Stat(path)
	return err == nil
}

// InitSessionMetadata 委托 session 包。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) InitSessionMetadata(params runtimepkg.InitMetadataParams) error {
	swarmsession.InitSessionMetadata(params.SessionID, params.ChannelID, params.UserID,
		params.Title, params.Mode, "")
	return nil
}

// SetSessionModelSelection 委托 session 包（当前无对应接口，空实现）。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) SetSessionModelSelection(string, map[string]any) error { return nil }

// HistoryExists 判定 history 文件是否存在。
func (*sessionOpsAdapter) HistoryExists(sessionID string) bool {
	path := filepath.Join(swarmsession.GetSessionsDir(), sessionID, "history.json")
	_, err := os.Stat(path)
	return err == nil
}

// BeginSessionDelete 桩：告警并放行。
func (noopTrajectoryHook) BeginSessionDelete(_ context.Context, sessionID string) error {
	logger.Warn(logComponent).Str("session_id", sessionID).
		Msg("trajectory delete not implemented (see 10.3.28 spec §10 R-3)")
	return nil
}

// AbortSessionDelete 桩。
func (noopTrajectoryHook) AbortSessionDelete(context.Context, string) error { return nil }

// CommitSessionDelete 桩。
func (noopTrajectoryHook) CommitSessionDelete(context.Context, string) error { return nil }

// UnbindSession 桩。
func (noopTeamBindingUnbinder) UnbindSession(teamName, sessionID string) error {
	logger.Warn(logComponent).Str("team_name", teamName).Str("session_id", sessionID).
		Msg("team binding unbind not implemented (see 10.3.28 spec §10 R-1)")
	return nil
}

// RecoveringDelete 桩：恒 false。
func (noopLifecycleJournal) RecoveringDelete(string) bool { return false }

// MarkCleanup 桩。
func (noopLifecycleJournal) MarkCleanup(string) error { return nil }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 写集成测试（4 用例，跨 5 个子系统）**

```go
//go:build integration

package server_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/suite"

	"github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/internal/common/config"
	pathutil "github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/internal/common/utils/path"
	"github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/internal/swarm/schema"
	"github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/internal/swarm/server/session"
	isuite "github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/tests/integration/suite"
	"github.com/uapclaw/uapclaw-go/tests/integration/testhelpers"
)

// SessionProvisionSuite 会话供给事务全链路：AgentServer 分发 → provisioner 事务
// → 会话元数据/历史 → 执行协调器 → 目录回收。
//
// 跨子系统：server / runtime / runtime.session / server.session / adapter 五者，
// 满足规则 7 的 ≥3 要求。
type SessionProvisionSuite struct {
	isuite.BaseIntegrationSuite
	// env 进程内 AgentServer 环境
	env *testhelpers.ServerEnv
	// dataDir 隔离数据根目录
	dataDir string
}

// TestSessionProvisionSuite 套件入口。
func TestSessionProvisionSuite(t *testing.T) {
	suite.Run(t, new(SessionProvisionSuite))
}

// SetupTest 隔离数据目录与会话缓存。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) SetupTest() {
	s.dataDir = s.T().TempDir()
	s.T().Setenv(pathutil.EnvDataDir, s.dataDir)
	pathutil.ResetCache()
	session.ClearAllSessionMetadataCache()
}

// TearDownTest 刷盘并还原环境。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) TearDownTest() {
	session.FlushMetadataQueue()
	session.ClearAllSessionMetadataCache()
	pathutil.ResetCache()
}

// SetupSuite 启动进程内 AgentServer。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) SetupSuite() {
	s.BaseIntegrationSuite.SetupSuite()
	cfg, err := config.New("")
	s.Require().NoError(err)
	s.env = testhelpers.NewServerEnv(s.T(), cfg, nil)
}

// TearDownSuite 关闭环境。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) TearDownSuite() {
	if s.env != nil {
		s.env.Close()
	}
	s.BaseIntegrationSuite.TearDownSuite()
}

// mustCreateSession 建一个会话并返回其标识（供各用例复用）。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) mustCreateSession(token string) string {
	s.T().Helper()
	requestID := s.env.Send(string(schema.ReqMethodSessionCreate), map[string]any{
		"create_token": token,
		"mode":         "agent",
		"work_mode":    "work",
	}, "", false)
	resp := s.env.AwaitUnary(requestID)
	s.Require().NotNil(resp, "create 应返回响应")
	s.Require().True(resp.OK, "create 应成功")
	payload := decodePayload(s.T(), resp)
	sessionID, _ := payload["session_id"].(string)
	s.Require().NotEmpty(sessionID, "create 应返回 session_id")
	return sessionID
}

// TestSessionProvisionCreate_全链路 校验 create 走完 prepare → commit。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) TestSessionProvisionCreate_全链路() {
	sessionID := s.mustCreateSession("integration-token")

	// 元数据已落盘
	meta := session.GetSessionMetadata(sessionID)
	s.Require().NotNil(meta, "create 后应有会话元数据")
	s.Require().Equal("agent", meta["mode"], "metadata 应记录归一化模式")
}

// TestSessionDelete_有执行在跑 校验先取消再删目录。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) TestSessionDelete_有执行在跑() {
	sessionID := s.mustCreateSession("tok-1")

	// 发起一次聊天（流式，不等完成），再删除
	s.env.Send(string(schema.ReqMethodChatSend), map[string]any{
		"query": "hello", "mode": "agent",
	}, sessionID, true)

	requestID := s.env.Send(string(schema.ReqMethodSessionDelete), map[string]any{
		"session_id": sessionID,
	}, sessionID, false)
	resp := s.env.AwaitUnary(requestID)
	s.Require().NotNil(resp, "删除应返回响应")

	payload := decodePayload(s.T(), resp)
	code, _ := payload["code"].(string)
	s.Require().Contains([]string{"", "DELETE_FAILED", "DELETE_COMMIT_PENDING"}, code,
		"删除结果码应为空（成功）或两个已知失败码之一")
	if resp.OK {
		s.Require().Empty(session.GetSessionMetadata(sessionID),
			"删除成功后元数据缓存应已清理")
	}
}

// TestSessionDelete_超时需恢复 校验超时时 recovery_required=true。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) TestSessionDelete_超时需恢复() {
	sessionID := s.mustCreateSession("tok-2")

	requestID := s.env.Send(string(schema.ReqMethodSessionDelete), map[string]any{
		"session_id": sessionID,
	}, sessionID, false)
	resp := s.env.AwaitUnary(requestID)
	s.Require().NotNil(resp)

	payload := decodePayload(s.T(), resp)
	if code, _ := payload["code"].(string); code == "DELETE_FAILED" {
		recovery, _ := payload["recovery_required"].(bool)
		deleted, _ := payload["deleted"].(bool)
		s.Require().Equal(deleted, recovery,
			"recovery_required 应等价于 deleted（破坏性已开始但失败）")
	}
}

// TestSessionSwitch_前台切换 校验切换链路。
func (s *SessionProvisionSuite) TestSessionSwitch_前台切换() {
	sessionID := s.mustCreateSession("tok-3")

	requestID := s.env.Send(string(schema.ReqMethodSessionSwitch), map[string]any{
		"session_id": sessionID,
		"mode":       "agent.plan",
		"view_id":    "integration-view",
	}, sessionID, false)
	resp := s.env.AwaitUnary(requestID)
	s.Require().NotNil(resp, "切换应返回响应")
	s.Require().True(resp.OK, "切换应成功")

	payload := decodePayload(s.T(), resp)
	s.Require().Equal(sessionID, payload["session_id"], "切换应返回同一会话")
	s.Require().Equal(true, payload["switched"])
}

// decodePayload 解析响应载荷。
func decodePayload(t *testing.T, resp *schema.AgentResponse) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	if resp == nil || len(resp.Payload) == 0 {
		return map[string]any{}
	}
	out := map[string]any{}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(resp.Payload, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("解析 payload 失败: %v", err)
	}
	return out
}
```

（`testhelpers.ServerEnv` 的接口：`Send(method string, params map[string]any, sessionID string, isStream bool) string` 返回 `request_id`；`AwaitUnary(requestID string) *schema.AgentResponse` 等待非流式响应；`AwaitStreamEnd(requestID) []*schema.AgentResponseChunk` 等待流结束。`Close()` 关闭环境。据此写代码，不要再做别的适配。）
